blink: Restrict TextCodecUtf8 simdutf fast path to x86_64 and arm64

third_party/simdutf only compiles its scalar fallback implementation on
32-bit targets (such as 32-bit ARM Android WebView) and non-SIMD 64-bit
targets, which regressed blink_perf.bindings TextDecoder benchmarks on
android-pixel4_webview-perf by ~11%.

Return false at compile time in TextCodecUtf8::IsSimdutfEnabled() when
SIMDUTF_IS_32BITS is defined or neither SIMDUTF_IS_X86_64 nor
SIMDUTF_IS_ARM64 is defined. This cannot be done via BASE_FEATURE
defaults or Finch cpu_architecture filters alone because
fieldtrial_testing_config.json lacks CPU-architecture filtering and
mixed-bitness Android WebView evaluates Finch filters in the 64-bit
host process before overriding FeatureList in the 32-bit renderer.

Revert CHECK to DCHECK in HidChooserContext::GetDeviceInfo

Revert the CHECK macro back to DCHECK in
HidChooserContext::GetDeviceInfo when verifying is_initialized_. When
GetDeviceInfo is called before the HID chooser context completes
initialization, is_initialized_ is false, which caused a crash in
release builds with CHECK enabled.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/9c6b992888400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

[3p freshness] libunwindstack: Roll libbase to android-17.0.0_r1

- libbase/crowbar.txtpb: add latest {} on the AOSP release tags
  (android-<N>.0.0_r<M>, a generic policy, as AOSP tags are not
  semver), the same policy as the libprocinfo roll
  (https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/q/I3a7bb6e9aab7dd5d93bd06ab18fe57bfa34c4a26),
  so the libunwindstack packages move to one Android release.
- Roll from a5c86f3574f7 to android-17.0.0_r1 (fef4173e19d9, 196
  commits) with crowbar resolve, promote and build --no-dry-run.
  not_valid_until is deleted: its bake-in delay is for CI rolls.
  README.chromium Version and Revision are what promote writes.
- 0003-remove-file-logging.patch is regenerated with the same changes:
  upstream added LOG_IF() right after LOG(), so its first hunk no
  longer applied.
- 0020-add-missing-includes.patch keeps only the logging.h include:
  upstream strings.h includes <iterator> since 4932431.
- What changes for Chromium: StringPrintf() and StringAppendV(), which
  libunwindstack and ART's dex code call, no longer use a temporary
  buffer for results of 1024 bytes or more (1fab24f). ReadFdToString()
  reads files of known size in one go (b5726dc, fc7353c); Chromium
  reads only /proc maps through it, whose size is 0, so the old loop
  still runs. Chromium does not call the rest: MappedFile::Create()
  (a5fcaab, which drops FromOsHandle()), a faster Join() (4932431),
  Readlink(), and the logging additions (LOG_IF(), TeeLogger(), a
  public severity table). LOG_IF() and TeeLogger() need logging.cpp,
  which BUILD.gn does not build.

  README.chromium Version and Revision are what promote writes.
- What changes for Chromium in process_map.h, the only file imported:
  MapInfo now turns MTE globals mappings ("[anon:mt:<name>+<offset>]")
  into that name and offset, and Maps.cpp reads /proc maps through
  MapInfo (ReadMapFile, ReadMapFileContent). The rest is new
  MappedFileSize() helpers, which Chromium does not call, and include
  fixes.

1d7efe05648be223c83ba49c194bfa664a638543 [ diff ] 
[Public Transit] Expect Activity destroyed in reachLastStop()

ActivityElement has no exit Condition by default because cross-Activity
Station transitions only stop the origin Activity in the background.
When exiting via reachLastStop() / reachLastStopAnd(), however, no
destination Station is entered, so leaving ActivityElement without an
exit Condition let reachLastStop() silently pass even while the
Station's Activity stayed RESUMED (unless the caller remembered to call
expectActivityDestroyed() beforehand, as all three existing callers
did).

Have reachLastStopAnd() call expectActivityDestroyed() on the exited
Station's ActivityElement when present, make expectActivityDestroyed()
idempotent for Stations that already set it in their constructor, and
drop the redundant manual calls before reachLastStop().

Validate SubmitForm URL and header in MediaAppPageHandler

MediaAppPageHandler::SubmitForm submits multipart form data to Lens.
This CL hardens the validation of that submission by ensuring that it
is securely transmitted to the correct server (by enforcing a strict
origin check rather than comparing only the host), and that the header
provided is valid, without `\r`, `\n`, or `\0` characters which could
confuse the server.

- 0020-add-missing-includes.patch is new: libunwindstack.git dcd4e94
  (2025) added #include <type_traits> to logging.h and <iterator> to
  strings.h in src/ by hand, with no patch. 0020 records that edit.
- libbase/README.chromium lists the patches and how to apply them.

Upstream a5c86f3574f7 plus libbase/patches/ gives libbase/src/:

    $ T=$(mktemp -d); L=third_party/libunwindstack/libbase
    $ git clone -q \
        https://android.googlesource.com/platform/system/libbase $T/up
    $ git -C $L ls-files src | while read f; do
    >   mkdir -p $T/$(dirname $f)
    >   git -C $T/up show a5c86f3574f7:${f#src/} > $T/$f
    > done
    $ (cd $T && git apply $OLDPWD/$L/patches/*.patch)
    $ diff -r $T/src $L/src && echo same
    same

Cancel prerender on unexpected or overlapping main-frame navigation
📋 Metadata
commit
2f5da3db4aacfe9280e5c3100b8dc02fdc8092d4
[ log ]
author
Jiacheng Guo <gjc@google.com>
Mon Nov 16 06:49:10 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Mon Nov 16 06:49:10 2026
tree
217123b51a5f5faabdd935856e100c8d6927f94d
parent
a9d5c36b3cdcf808cf1b42957a1d3bc40892f40e [ diff ] 
Cancel prerender on unexpected or overlapping main-frame navigation

Under RenderDocument and navigation queueing, a second cross-document
main-frame navigation can start in a prerendered page while a previous
navigation is pending commit. When the first navigation finishes, it
sets is_ready_for_activation_ to true while the second is in flight,
causing a CHECK(!is_ready_for_activation_) crash on the second finish.

Track the active expected main-frame navigation ID on PrerenderHost,
cancel prerendering immediately when main-frame navigations overlap or
an unexpected navigation finishes, and record the new final status
PrerenderFinalStatus::kUnexpectedMainFrameNavigation.

[ log ]
author
ayugoto <ayugoto@google.com>
Mon Nov 16 06:47:20 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Mon Nov 16 06:47:20 2026
tree
ff6aa9fd29001070c0fab36c30a7bbb444deaad1
parent
8eb92fb1eda51545cb3e4c33a8edd9bc7ade4d6d [ diff ] 
[TopChrome] Hide GetRegisteredConfig() behind GetProcessLockHost()

After decoupling Top Chrome WebUI process sharing from `.top-chrome`
URL strings (https://crrev.com/c/8689185),
`WebUIConfigMap::GetRegisteredConfig()` is only used to read
`WebUIConfig::process_lock_host()` for process model checks. Exposing
`GetRegisteredConfig()` publicly makes it easy for callers to
inadvertently use it instead of `GetConfig()` and bypass
`IsWebUIEnabled()` checks.

This CL makes `GetRegisteredConfig()` private and exposes
`WebUIConfigMap::GetProcessLockHost()`, which returns only the process
lock host of the registered config, for process model lookups.

This CL does not affect the behavior.

[ServiceWorker] Queue startup during network restriction registration
📋 Metadata
commit
5650a37cd3a6f5207f179a861411b82437c1b319
[ log ]
author
Shunya Shishido <sisidovski@chromium.org>
Mon Nov 16 06:44:56 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Mon Nov 16 06:44:56 2026
tree
d1a20841d5cab0c79536ebde87d3030b2d447461
parent
0f3271f353b84ffdd542b91d994e0ecb0a3d19e4 [ diff ] 
[ServiceWorker] Queue startup during network restriction registration

A stopped worker's network restrictions (connection allowlists) are
registered with the network service before the worker starts.
MaybeRegisterNetworkRestrictions() marked them as registered when it
sent the IPC, not when the reply arrived, and the worker stayed kStopped
in between. A second StartWorker() in that window skipped the wait and
started the worker. The reply to the first request then ran
StartWorkerInternal() on a worker that was no longer stopped. That
replaced `worker_host_`, and destroying the previous ServiceWorkerHost
can release the last reference to the version (held by one of its
ServiceWorkerObjectHosts), deleting the version while
StartWorkerInternal() was still running on it.

Mark the restrictions as registered only when the network service
replies, and queue callers that arrive before that. Wait for the
registration at the top of DidEnsureLiveRegistrationForStartWorker(),
before any start state is touched. Once the registration is done the
queued requests run the existing start logic in order, so the first one
starts the worker and the others find it kStarting. Waiting there also
keeps concurrent WARM_UP requests from calling
EmbeddedWorkerInstance::SetPauseInitializingGlobalScope() twice, which
CHECKs.

Queued requests have not reached `start_callbacks_`, so the destructor
aborts them and HasWorkInBrowser() counts them. The reply callback also
runs if the network context is disconnected before replying, so the
queue is not left waiting forever; StoragePartitionImpl re-sends the
restrictions to the next network context.

The new ServiceWorkerVersionTest cases cover concurrent, WARM_UP,
doomed, destroyed and disconnected requests during the registration.

e5660b862cc889b9dc9316035408d52ff3b5a6ee
parent
44c12164a5b472429579dcaa50e50db3e3015b14 [ diff ] 
WebNN: Support uint4 constant input for dequantizeLinear

SDXL Turbo uses uint4 weights for dequantizeLinear, but TFLite has no
uint4 tensor type, so the TFLite backend rejected uint4 operands.

A uint4 constant can be written as int4 by subtracting 8 from each value
In SerializeOperand(). Subtracting 8 from a 4-bit value is the same as
flipping its high bit.

Mon Nov 16 06:15:21 2026
tree
d7947edd46cb182d76136b8b21ca33e387f9e7a6
parent
fe911d8d2da03d1258aebe601d1c1928836938f6 [ diff ] 
[PA] Make SchedulerLoopQuarantine's dequarantine loop generic

`SchedulerLoopQuarantineBranch::PurgeInternal()` hard-codes where the
dequarantined slots that the thread cache does not take go: into a
`BatchFreeQueue`, which frees them under the partition lock.

Move the loop into a lambda parameterized by the destination queue and
by the thread cache fill policy, and wrap the lazily constructed
`BatchFreeQueue` in `LazyBatchFreeQueue` to make it one such
destination. A follow-up adds a second destination, a queue drained by
another thread, without duplicating the loop.

The fill policy is now chosen once per purge rather than once per slot.
No behavior change.

`SchedulerLoopQuarantinePerfTest` shows no regression from this CL and
its parent together (Linux, AMD EPYC 7B13, 9 runs interleaved with the
commit before them, `browser_main` and `browser_main_unlimited`
configurations): the median change per scenario is between -4.1% and
+1.9%, and no 95% confidence interval lies entirely above zero.

72e1cac5261799ca26fef097af03d71e51198781 [ diff ] 
[Media Router] Replace OffscreenTab::Owner with a destroy callback

OffscreenTab used the Owner interface only to ask its owner to destroy
it. Replace the interface with a base::OnceClosure passed to the
constructor, and remove OffscreenTab::Owner.

The bug suggested holding OffscreenTab by a WeakPtr, which would make
OffscreenTab self-owned. Keeping ownership with the existing owners
and running a callback removes the interface without introducing a
self-deleting object.

No behavior change.

c880da5dde1073cd97806fb3999ed7dbe2b8209e

tree
3f9340c39c99f995b7fa78a46f51d0be061b5cde
parent
6405ba4afd05f88765c75e3570ce1f7e51957e46 [ diff ] 
Linux: Keep the active tab distinct from the frame in dark GTK themes

With "Use GTK" on libadwaita-style dark themes such as Adwaita-dark and
Yaru-dark, the active tab and toolbar use the GTK window background,
which is barely different from the focused headerbar (1.14:1 on
Yaru-dark) and identical to the unfocused headerbar (1:1), so the
active tab can't be told apart from the tab strip.

When the native frame colors are dark, lighten the toolbar until it
reaches a 1.3:1 contrast ratio against both frame colors, the same
threshold BrowserView::ShouldDrawTabStrokes() uses (now shared as
kMinimumActiveTabContrastRatio). The active tab background now always
uses kColorToolbar instead of the GTK/Qt supplied color, so the two
can't diverge. Light frames and toolbars that already have enough
contrast are unchanged. The toolbar top separator now follows the
adjusted toolbar color.

Fixed: 456671384

tree
df55517412cda4f69506681c26af9f2963521335
parent
a91c16360a14b30345d099ea99c3a55e034cdd53 [ diff ] 
policy: Stop 279 test files from compiling the cloud policy proto header

Two policy test helpers pulled the generated cloud policy proto header
(4.9 MB) into 344 test files of the ChromeOS build. After this change,
279 of those files no longer compile it.

That is about 1.4 GB less generated header text and about 4.5
CPU-minutes less compile time per full build, with each affected file
compiling 6.5% faster. It also means 279 fewer files to rebuild
whenever the cloud policy protos change.

The helpers now forward-declare the proto type instead of including it.

Test: autoninja -k 0 on affected objects; components_unittests
Bug: None

parent
cb525a8b388d2f2339048386ac553043ea8d21e8 [ diff ] 
[PartitionAlloc] Fix `hardening_unittest.cc` without moved metadata

`HardeningTest.PoolOffsetMetadataPointerCrashing` passes an
`UntaggedSlotStart` to `FreelistEntry::EmplaceAndInitForTest()`, which
takes a `uintptr_t`, in its `!PA_CONFIG(MOVE_METADATA_OUT_OF_GIGACAGE)`
branch (missed in https://crrev.com/c/7140801). That branch is only
compiled when `PA_USE_DEATH_TESTS()` is true and the metadata is not
moved out of the GigaCage, a combination that no configuration has built
so far (https://crbug.com/585944271). It breaks the build of
`base_unittests` on the iOS Simulator once GoogleTest death tests are
enabled there.

Pass `slot_start.value()`, as the other branch already does.

Test: base_unittests --gtest_filter=HardeningTest.*

tree
a89e5dbb1cdb99589375c68f216c0cc1b9385aa4
parent
62ad4d4a23a2ca42619951b1d15c52a139ed34b8 [ diff ] 
Ash: Make the ARC data removal dialog own its widget

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to move Ash widgets off
the deprecated NATIVE_WIDGET_OWNS_WIDGET and WIDGET_OWNS_NATIVE_WIDGET
ownership modes and onto CLIENT_OWNS_WIDGET, the mode views recommends
for all widgets. With it, the code that creates a widget owns it and
decides when it is destroyed. This removes a common source of dangling
pointers and window teardown bugs.

A static slot now owns the data removal dialog's widget instead of
keeping a raw pointer to the dialog, and destroys the widget when the
dialog is closed or its window goes away.

87b42e62279486bb1e79c9bfc44282b06c8a12e6
parent
d874b03d1983d3467687fadf29e93fecdd352054 [ diff ] 
FSA: validate source entry type before moving or renaming

Previously, `FileSystemAccessHandleBase::Move()` and `Rename()` did not
verify that the source entry on disk still matched the handle's type
(such as replacing a regular file with a directory), allowing a stale
FileSystemFileHandle to rename a directory and only checking destination
permissions and blocklist rules as `HandleType::kFile`.

Add `GetHandleType()` to FileSystemAccessHandleBase and its subclasses,
pass it to `GetRenamePermission()` and `RunWithSensitiveEntryAccess()`,
and verify under the exclusive move locks that `url()` still exists as a
matching file or directory before performing the move.

Original change's description:
> [Variations] Add GlicAndroidTablet to the fieldtrial testing config
>
> Adds the GlicAndroidTablet study (Gemini in Chrome on Android tablets)
> to the field trial testing config so perf bots test it, matching the
> Finch Enabled_20261110 group.
>
> The GlicAndroid and EnableAndroidSidePanel entries had no form factor
> filter, so they also applied to tablets, and the same features can't be
> set by two studies on one client. Scope both to desktop, foldable and
> phone so tablets get these features only from GlicAndroidTablet. This
> also stops tablet bots from running GlicAndroid's phone-only features
> (AndroidBottomBar, TabBottomSheet).
>
> Adds "foldable" to the form factors that fieldtrial_to_struct.py
> accepts (Study::FOLDABLE), so foldables keep these entries.
>
> No-Presubmit: CheckDuplicatedFeatures in testing/variations/PRESUBMIT.py
> reports EnableAndroidSidePanel and GlicActor as set by two studies, but it
> ignores form_factors: GlicAndroidTablet is tablet-only and the other two
> entries are now desktop/foldable/phone-only, so they never overlap. The
> check's docstring says to bypass it in this case.
>

tree
7ca334463f12a360f1d6e25107ca9c656c538893
parent
7702f1d07d691adfa5beef8fc179db5704cc8c03 [ diff ] 
Reject the open directory mode in FileChooserImpl

FileChooserImpl::OpenFileChooser() rejects kSave from the renderer, but
not kOpenDirectory, which Blink never sends. FileSelectHelper doesn't
handle kOpenDirectory and hits NOTREACHED(), so a compromised renderer
can crash the browser. Only the File System Access API uses
kOpenDirectory, in the browser process for Android WebView, bypassing
this Mojo interface.

Reject kOpenDirectory like kSave.

Bug: 586101387

tree
cb8f1f005657068421154b718b1b1229645db468
parent
17ba7a0067e02c76f95b74344571788981ce0138 [ diff ] 
NavigationInitiator: Plumb upfront NavigationUIData through OpenURLParams and Prerender

Overview doc:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/13J0F_tXGXODwd13myA9R5exfz3VrbnNlSzs1NNGcoyo/edit?usp=sharing

Extend content::NavigationUIData with two lifecycle hooks and plumb
upfront NavigationUIData through OpenURLParams and
WebContents::StartPrerendering():

- NavigationUIData::PopulateFromNavigationHandle(NavigationHandle&):
  Called in NavigationRequest::StartNavigation() when upfront
  NavigationUIData was supplied, before DidStartNavigation() fires,
  without allocating NavigationUIData on untagged non-network
  navigations (which continue to allocate lazily in
  NavigationRequest::WillStartRequest()).
- NavigationUIData::CloneForNewNavigation(): Called by
  OpenURLParams::FromNavigationHandle() to clone upfront caller inputs
  while stripping per-NavigationHandle state (returning nullptr by
  default for subclasses without upfront fields).
- Plumb upfront NavigationUIData through OpenURLParams,
  LoadURLParams(const OpenURLParams&), WebContents::StartPrerendering(),
  and PrerenderAttributes into the initial prerender NavigationRequest.

No behavioral change.

Co-Authored-By: Ken Okada <kenoss@chromium.org>

tree
c1fff5fcba0e28f4005819a4b575f7df2c94b361
parent
1a58591025948c44d9c289ec91dcce5e7af05a91 [ diff ] 
[PDF] Isolate SearchOpenParam test from desktop size

`PDFExtensionTest.SearchOpenParam` fails deterministically on bots with
tall displays such as `win11-arm64-rel-tests`. In `test-bookmarks.pdf`,
"Subsection" sits near the top of page 1 (`y` ~ 1233..1253 CSS px at
100% zoom). When the default browser viewport height exceeds ~1253 px,
`PDFiumEngine::ScrollToBoundingRects()` sees the match already inside
`GetVisibleRect()` at `scrollTop = 0` and returns early without
scrolling, leaving page 0 as the most visible page until `RunUntil()`
times out.

Resize `WebContents` to 1024x768 before loading the PDF, matching
`PDFExtensionScrollTest`, so page 1 starts off-screen regardless of the
host monitor resolution. Keeping the test on `PDFExtensionTest` rather
than `PDFExtensionScrollTest` preserves the test ID and avoids adding
`--disable-smooth-scrolling`.

parent
d9d4373236e95b9690ca15b59eb1a26a0e8d421d [ diff ] 
[PA] Replace allow_clear_bucket with ThreadCache::CacheFillPolicy

`ThreadCache::MaybePutInCache()` has a `bool allow_clear_bucket`
template parameter choosing what happens when a bucket fills up. Turn
it into an enum, `CacheFillPolicy`, with one value per behavior:
`kClearOnOverflow` (was `true`) and `kCapAtHalfLimit` (was `false`).

A follow-up adds a third policy, for asynchronous purging of
`SchedulerLoopQuarantine`, which a bool cannot express.

No behavior change.

parent
32a38dcac4f47d540bf410f1b8b8afbab77b0fb1 [ diff ] 
Make SyncLoadContext owned by the blocked caller in SendSync

Previously, SyncLoadContext was self-owned on the background loading
thread via ResourceRequestSender::PendingRequestInfo and exposed to
the blocked caller thread via a raw SyncLoadContext** out-parameter.

Refactor SyncLoadContext so the blocked caller holds a scoped_refptr
across SendSync() and releases it on the loading task runner via
ReleaseSoon(), removing the SyncLoadContext** context_for_redirect
out-parameter.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Defer SyncLoadContext completion while handling redirects
📋 Metadata
commit
32a38dcac4f47d540bf410f1b8b8afbab77b0fb1
[ log ]
author
Jiacheng Guo <gjc@google.com>
Mon Nov 16 04:40:16 2026
committer

Mon Nov 16 04:40:16 2026
tree
dd25f8ea955986e8aac1f673a5770394e4682352
parent
f16cdc66b5e2b377e009366c5eddf09d4e762ad8 [ diff ] 
Defer SyncLoadContext completion while handling redirects

When SyncLoadContext receives a redirect during a synchronous load, it
hands control to the blocked calling thread to inspect the redirect
before posting FollowRedirect() or CancelRedirect(). If the request
completes or disconnects on the background thread during that window,
completing immediately mutates SyncLoadResponse concurrently and tears
down SyncLoadContext while the calling thread still references it.

Defer OnCompletedRequest() into deferred_completion_status_ while a
redirect callback is pending, and apply or clear the deferred status
once FollowRedirect() or CancelRedirect() runs. Also enforce redirect
state invariants with CHECKs and add unit tests.

tree
8b0b97b6d9524984f86e054b3ded43701f535820
parent
9b13bf92cd71df30d63814793cf03d618404056e [ diff ] 
blink: Replace <cstdint> with <stdint.h> in renderer/platform/

The Google C++ Style Guide states that fixed-width integer types should
not use `std::`, and `<cstdint>` is not guaranteed to provide them
without `std::` (unlike `<stdint.h>`).

Remove `#include <cstdint>` in `third_party/blink/renderer/platform/`,
include `<stdint.h>` where needed, and drop remaining `std::` prefixes.

This CL has no behavior changes.

This CL adds getGPayLogoDrawableId to AutofillUiUtils.java and calls it
where it's needed. This helper function aims to be the single place
where all Google Pay logos are retrieved from on the Java side, though
certain icons passed from the C++ side aren't handled by it.

[ReadAloud] Coordinate playback with audible page media

Pause native Read Aloud playback when any audible HTML media element on
the page begins playing to prevent overlapping audio. In addition, stop
and evict active page media from MediaSession when Read Aloud playback
is started or resumed to avoid concurrent playback (or dual-resume) via
OS media controls.

[Variations] Add GlicAndroidTablet to the fieldtrial testing config

Adds the GlicAndroidTablet study (Gemini in Chrome on Android tablets)
to the field trial testing config so perf bots test it, matching the
Finch Enabled_20261110 group.

The GlicAndroid and EnableAndroidSidePanel entries had no form factor
filter, so they also applied to tablets, and the same features can't be
set by two studies on one client. Scope both to desktop, foldable and
phone so tablets get these features only from GlicAndroidTablet. This
also stops tablet bots from running GlicAndroid's phone-only features
(AndroidBottomBar, TabBottomSheet).

Adds "foldable" to the form factors that fieldtrial_to_struct.py
accepts (Study::FOLDABLE), so foldables keep these entries.

No-Presubmit: CheckDuplicatedFeatures in testing/variations/PRESUBMIT.py
reports EnableAndroidSidePanel and GlicActor as set by two studies, but it
ignores form_factors: GlicAndroidTablet is tablet-only and the other two
entries are now desktop/foldable/phone-only, so they never overlap. The
check's docstring says to bypass it in this case.

[Actor] Merge duplicate reset paths in the actor control state tracker

The tracker repeated its reset and notify logic in four places: an
incognito tab, an invalid profile, a newly attached profile, and a
missing Glic helper. The task lookup already returns nothing when the
helper or service is missing, so all four now go through the shared
instance change handler with the same observer calls.

Revert CHECK_EQ to DCHECK_EQ in ElementTracker::GetUniqueElement

The CHECK_EQ macro in ui::ElementTracker::GetUniqueElement causes
crashes when there are multiple elements matching the specified
identifier and context. This reverts the CHECK_EQ to a DCHECK_EQ to
avoid altering non-debug behavior and adds the required TODO comment.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/5b0a02c208400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

parent
2ca076eb496ed7534393ee8ff4d6a55d20c72393 [ diff ] 
libjingle_xmpp: Accept Expat's own XML declaration version error in test

Expat 2.8.5 rejects an XML declaration version other than 1.x itself,
with XML_ERROR_XML_DECL (upstream libexpat #1354). XmlParserTest.TestError
expected XML_ERROR_SYNTAX for <?xml version="2.0"?>, which only
libjingle's own check in XmlParser::ExpatXmlDecl() raises, so the test
fails with Expat 2.8.5 or newer.

Expect XML_ERROR_XML_DECL when building against Expat 2.8.5 or newer,
and XML_ERROR_SYNTAX otherwise, since Linux and ChromeOS builds use the
system Expat, which can be older. XmlParser itself is unchanged: such
documents are still rejected.

This is needed before rolling third_party/expat to R_2_9_0.

tree
b220c0050a90b238d983da667e11d770bac5bf60
parent
ac0b52278effbdc3787d10f1065d3e59b32784f4 [ diff ] 
session_manager: Introduce SessionManagerObserver::OnActiveSessionSwitched

As a step to merge UserManager::UserSessionStateObserver into
SessionManagerObserver, this CL introduces
SessionManagerObserver::OnActiveSessionSwitched(), which is the
replacement of UserSessionStateObserver::ActiveUserChanged().

During the migration period, SessionManager observes
UserSessionStateObserver and forwards ActiveUserChanged() to
OnActiveSessionSwitched() with the corresponding Session. It is
CHECKed that the Session exists for the active user, so tests that
log in or switch the active user by directly calling
UserManager::UserLoggedIn() or SwitchActiveUser() while SessionManager
observes the UserManager are updated to go through
SessionManager::CreateSession() (or UserSessionTestEnvironment::LogIn())
instead.

Note that OnActiveSessionSwitched() is invoked at the timing when
SessionManager receives ActiveUserChanged(). Because SessionManager
starts observing UserManager right after UserManager is created, it
is notified earlier than other UserSessionStateObservers.

As the first caller, NoteTakingControllerClient is migrated.

Bug: 278643115
Test: components_unittests --gtest_filter=SessionManagerTest.*
Test: unit_tests, chromeos_unittests, components_unittests

ec10344e2a32a2401b22dec4aec119305b502bd8
parent
4e7bb08b1f9c69a44ca06817be6d5deb901c0e5c [ diff ] 
[TopChrome] Decouple WebUI process sharing from .top-chrome URL suffix

Top Chrome WebUIs currently rely on a `.top-chrome` hostname suffix and
a Chrome-specific pseudo-TLD extraction hack in `SiteInfo::Create()` to
share a renderer process, coupling WebUI URLs to internal
process-sharing mechanics.

This CL replaces the `.top-chrome` TLD parsing and two-call
`effective_url` hack in `SiteInfo::Create()` and
`ChromeContentBrowserClient::ShouldTryToUseExistingProcessHost()` with
`WebUIConfig`-based queries:
- Add an optional `process_lock_host` constructor argument to
  `WebUIConfig`, stored as a `const` member and validated at
  construction, so the process lock host cannot change for the lifetime
  of a config. Add `WebUIConfigMap::GetRegisteredConfig()` to look it up
  without evaluating `IsWebUIEnabled()`.
- Make `WebUIConfigMap` CHECK that a process lock host is not the host
  of a registered `chrome://` WebUI, and that `RemoveConfig()` is not
  used for a config with a process lock host. Tests remove such
  configs through `RemoveConfigForTesting()`, which
  `ScopedWebUIConfigRegistration` uses.
- Add `TopChromeWebUIConfig::ProcessModel`, which defaults to
  `kTopChromeProcess` (process lock host `"top-chrome"` for trusted
  `chrome://` WebUIs). Existing non-`.top-chrome` subclasses pass
  `kDedicatedProcess`, and `DiceWebSigninInterceptUIConfig` (which uses
  `DefaultWebUIConfig` with a `.top-chrome` URL) passes `"top-chrome"`
  directly.

This CL does not affect the behavior.

Top Chrome WebUIs (Reading List, Organizer, Omnibox popup) share one
renderer process while chrome://version/ runs in a separate one.

Test: Tested locally by confirming in chrome://process-internals that

[🌷] Deflake ChromeItemPickerActivityTest

ChromeItemPickerActivityTest#testActivityLaunchWithExplicitWindowId
and testActivityLaunchWithoutWindowId_resolvesFromSameTaskHost launch
ChromeItemPickerActivity without finishing the activity lifecycle via
ApplicationTestUtils.finishActivity(activity). During test runner
teardown, the unmanaged activity's DecorView is retained by the
Android framework InputMethodManager, causing
LeakCanaryChecker.checkLeaks() to fail with
NoLeakAssertionFailedError in @AfterClass.

Extract launch and creation synchronization into a reusable fixture
helper `launchPickerActivity` and guarantee activity destruction in a
finally block via `ApplicationTestUtils.finishActivity(activity)`.

UTR: 63/63 passed on android-x64-rel (chrome_public_test_apk,
*ChromeItemPickerActivityTest*) —
https://chromium-swarm.appspot.com/task?id=7bf57898b81a9a10

Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:android-x64-rel

44903fba40620c0a9aa6e8e94926117880298d70 [ diff ] 
Handle DOM mutations during text replace in editing commands

Synchronous event listeners can run while replacing text in a node and
mutate or disconnect the involved text and selection endpoints. Copy the
replacement string before mutating the DOM, clamp updated offsets, and
abort the editing command if selection endpoints become invalid.

Fixed: 576742430

5c6203cf0f9371b0ad2245ced292619e87003044 [ diff ] 
Preserve fetched response headers for identical service worker scripts

When a service worker update finds that one script changed while another
script body remained byte-for-byte identical, the unchanged script is
copied into the new version instead of being re-written from network
bytes. Previously, the copy path also preserved the old version's stored
response head, which kept stale HTTP headers such as
Content-Security-Policy on the new service worker version.

Preserve the newly fetched URLResponseHead during update checking and
pass it through to the pause-and-copy path so copied scripts keep the
existing body bytes while writing the updated response headers.

Bug: 578654292

tree
c2dfc7c85a148b20311ff10c96d82f5efd861319
parent
691faf2e02a1d71f636bebc9e33417f9033a92e7 [ diff ] 
blink: Exclude .cc-only generators from core:all_generators

Currently, //third_party/blink/renderer/core:all_generators exposes all
46 generator targets in targets_generating_sources via public_deps.
However, 7 of those targets (:make_core_generated_cssom_types,
:make_core_generated_event_factory,
:make_core_generated_property_bitsets,
:make_core_generated_web_origin_trials,
:make_core_generated_permissions_policy_helper,
:make_core_generated_css_primitive_value_unit_trie, and
:make_core_generated_html_entity_table) only generate .cc files and
produce no headers.

Because :all_generators is in public_deps of :core_generated and
blink_core_sources, :make_core_generated_cssom_types (~58s remote exec,
finishing at ~63.7s on clean builds) becomes an order-only dependency
(.harddeps) of 15,690 C++ compilation steps across Blink, content, and
chrome, even though the slowest header-producing generator in
:all_generators (:make_core_generated_computed_style_base) finishes ~26s
earlier (~37.3s).

Split targets_generating_sources by checking whether get_target_outputs
contains any "*.h" files, and move the .cc-only generators onto a
headerless blink_core_sources("core_generated_cc_only") target with
public = [] (following the existing :font_resource pattern) in private
deps of component("core"). This removes the .cc-only generator actions
from core.harddeps and unblocks downstream C++ compilation 26s earlier.

ash: Add missing extension API generated header deps

After crrev.com/c/8779493 and crrev.com/c/8779634 removed
//chrome/common from ash targets for crbug.com/332804822,
linux-chromeos-build-perf-siso reports 11 missing dependency errors on
generated headers from //chromeos/ash/experiences/extensions/common/api
(file_system_provider.h, file_system_provider_internal.h,
file_manager_private.h) and //chrome/common/extensions/api
(settings_private.h).

Because //chrome/browser/ash/file_manager,
//chrome/browser/ash/file_system_provider,
//chrome/browser/ash/extensions/file_manager, and
//chrome/browser/extensions have circular dependencies with these five
ash targets via allow_circular_includes_from, add the generated
extension API targets directly to deps.

tree
82761eccfdd66bf26fb7fa6c26c8bb55acd77a63
parent
efa6ce2119f432f0eba0d27fab69298c33b3fd25 [ diff ] 
Pack Element on 64-bit Windows to shrink SVG elements

The MSVC ABI does not place derived class members in base class tail
padding. Element is 8-byte aligned only because of tag_name_, so on
64-bit Windows each SVG element subclass that adds only 32-bit members
is padded to 8 bytes at each level.

Pack Element to 4 bytes on 64-bit Windows. This doesn't move any of its
members, and a static_assert checks that tag_name_ stays 8-byte aligned.
Other platforms, including 32-bit Windows, are unchanged.

Savings per element on 64-bit Windows (allocation size, which Oilpan
rounds up to 8 bytes):
  16 bytes: path, polyline, polygon, circle, text, tspan
   8 bytes: g, use, image, svg, a, rect, ellipse, line, clipPath,
            symbol, defs, foreignObject
No element grows.

tree
ae70d8b0f7e3f49d55140850d8815166f0edb83d
parent
0c9aa04aac6bd35cb825bb31b9c269b118b2cfaa [ diff ] 
[iOS] Pass callback by const ref in `ConsistencyPromoSigninMediator`

A limitation of OCMock limits the type of arguments of the methods it
can mock. In particular, it is not possible to pass a RepeatingCallback
or even a non-const reference to a repeating callback. However, const
references are acceptable. It’s actually a better implementation than
pointer, as it’s clear that it can’t be nullptr and we don’t need to
CHECK_DEREF its value.

Bug: none

tree
380d063eae640147e2b92fd6f4fba926f668c35d
parent
4aa37bbe950c2a2b1d8ee6dae1f4a3ad47a91ad3 [ diff ] 
[ServiceWorker] Check NavigationPreloadRequest lifetime in callbacks

In NavigationPreloadRequest::OnReceiveRedirect() and OnComplete(),
OnNavigationPreloadResponse() is invoked prior to
OnNavigationPreloadComplete(). Resolving the preload response promise
can synchronously execute script, which may trigger worker context
destruction and delete the request object while it is still on the call
stack.

Introduce a WeakPtr check to ensure the NavigationPreloadRequest
instance is still valid before calling OnNavigationPreloadComplete(). In
addition, verify that context_ is non-null in ServiceWorkerContextClient
prior to removing preload requests in OnNavigationPreloadError() and
OnNavigationPreloadComplete().

tree
4fee0e951317e20b0dd8e45de440dfcdd9dab9fb
parent
7aaddee8f30a67e10e2a2ee10b5fb2fbf1db70db [ diff ] 
[ozone/wayland] Make tooltips of bubbles subsurfaces

Tooltips parented to a bubble were created as xdg_popups whose xdg
parent is the bubble's xdg_toplevel, since the bubble itself is a
wl_subsurface. xdg_positioner doesn't allow the anchor rect to extend
outside the parent's window geometry, and a popup must intersect or
be adjacent to its parent. When the bubble extends past the toplevel
(e.g. Page Info in a narrow picture-in-picture window), Mutter doesn't
show the tooltip.

Tooltips don't take input or need a grab, so create them as
subsurfaces of the bubble instead, like nested bubbles. Menus still
use xdg_popup since they need a grab.

Prevent inserting <div> in invalid hosts

This patch modifies InsertParagraphSeparatorCommand to insert a line
break instead of a <div> when the host cannot accept it. Blocks nested
inside such hosts are now split as usual instead of getting a line
break. This behavior is guarded by the feature flag
InsertParagraphInEditingHostCannotHaveDiv.

parent
ad991a13ca8199fa11f1b7bb1f9164a611d568fa [ diff ] 
[Actor] Attach autofill and password clients to headless pages

Headless actor pages are bare WebContents with no tab helpers, so actor
tools that need them fail. For example, ChangePasswordTool fails with
kFrameWentAway because there is no password manager driver.

A tab gets these helpers from TabFeatures. ActorSurfaceRegistry now
attaches the same autofill and password manager clients when it creates
a headless surface, before the page's first navigation. This covers
ChangePasswordTool, AttemptLoginTool and AttemptFormFillingTool.

436a296b8e1760e9cc3a9ed1a7099a723d0f3ab9 [ diff ] 
[WebApps] Reuse `IDS_WEB_APP_WITH_APP_TITLE` for page-title windows

Hard-coding "-" or any other punctuation is not proper i18n. In this
case however, the actual way to merge app name and page title already
exists in the codebase so we can just reuse it.

No change for en-us users, as the punctuation is the same. This changes
the result for en-GB users for example since translators used an
unspaced dash. And generally speaking, that follows the translators'
choice for titles.

Bug: 579665324, 586195412

Plumb fallback IPv4 address through AsyncDnsJob and EndpointConnector

Plumb an alternative IPv4 address through QuicSessionPool::EndpointConnector
and QuicSessionPool::AsyncDnsJob into QuicChromiumClientSession for
IPv6-to-IPv4 connection migration.

This is necessary to support IPv6-to-IPv4 QUIC connection migration. See
https://crrev.com/c/8550833.

Add IPv6-to-IPv4 QUIC connection migration support

Add support for migrating a QUIC connection from an IPv6 path to an
alternative IPv4-only network in QuicChromiumClientSession behind the
kEnableQuicV6ToV4Migration feature flag, along with
QuicMigrationAttemptContext tracking and migration histograms.

When this happens, we should migrate to an IPv4 address that routes to a
"server" that has the state necessary to handle the migration. No RFC
currently defines how to come up with an IPv4 address that guarantees
that.
https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/draft-munizaga-quic-alternative-server-address/
is a start, but it's still a draft and requires implementing new frames.
Until that is done, we approximate this IPv4 by using the IPv4 address
we got during the original hostname resolution that led to the creation
of the connection we're trying to migrate to an IPv4-only network. This
is not perfect, but it's an initial approximation that lets us land the
mechanism that will also be used by any long-term solution.

Note: this CL only adds the migration logic in
QuicChromiumClientSession; set_fallback_ipv4_peer_address() has no
callers yet. Plumbing the DNS-resolved IPv4 address into the session
happens in follow-up CLs.

0949b08246e266110f34b372f9cc96a6b1407a48 [ diff ] 
[updater] Add proxy, cancellation and timeouts to the libcurl fetcher

LibcurlNetworkFetcher takes an optional explicit proxy in libcurl
syntax. When set, it overrides the proxy environment (empty
CURLOPT_NOPROXY) and keeps the CONNECT reply out of the header
callback, so a caller can point the fetcher at a proxy without relying
on the process environment.

Downloads also gain the behavior the NetworkFetcher contract expects:
- redirects are followed (up to 5) and the final status, not the 3xx,
  is reported as the response code;
- the response code is reported even when libcurl never learns the body
  length (no body, chunked, or a redirect chain), which previously left
  the response-started callback unfired for a bodiless 404;
- the closure returned by DownloadToFile cancels the transfer through
  the progress callback, and the download completes with
  net::ERR_ABORTED as components/update_client/network.h specifies;
- CURLOPT_NOSIGNAL plus connect and low-speed timeouts bound how long
  an unresponsive endpoint can hold a transfer in a multithreaded
  process.

The Linux factory still passes no proxy, so it only picks up the fixes
above. Unit tests cover each behavior with an embedded test server
acting as origin and as HTTP proxy.

[Mac] Fix race in `InputTextPreventedSyncsCursorLocation`

`RenderWidgetHostViewMacTest.InputTextPreventedSyncsCursorLocation` was
disabled on macOS due to flakiness.

After navigating and calling `ExecJs()` to focus `<textarea id=ta>`, the
test immediately dispatched `[rwhv_cocoa keyEvent:key_a]` without:
1. Focusing the primary main frame and waiting for the asynchronous
   `TextInputStateChanged` update to set `rwhv_cocoa.textInputType` to
   `ui::TEXT_INPUT_TYPE_TEXT_AREA`, which is required for `[rwhv_cocoa
   inputContext]` to be non-nil during `[rwhv_cocoa
   interpretKeyEvents:]`.
2. Activating the widget's `InputRouter` via `MakeActive()`, matching
   the other `keyEvent:` tests in the same file, so
   `RenderInputRouter::FilterInputEvent()` does not drop the forwarded
   key events while post-navigation paint holding is active.

Focus the primary main frame, wait for `rwhv_cocoa.textInputType` to
reach `ui::TEXT_INPUT_TYPE_TEXT_AREA`, activate the `InputRouter` before
sending the key event, and re-enable the test. Also remove the unused
local class `InputMethodObserver` from the test.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

[Wayland] Apply text-input-v3 deletions to the tracked text state

41142f41c14530dbc09b231a4ab1a2e384307a72
parent
6d3000feae9b387f865bae8d89d1caa3ca688592 [ diff ] 
[Wayland] Apply text-input-v3 deletions to the tracked text state

text-input-v3 specifies delete_surrounding_text lengths relative to the
current cursor, excluding the selection. ZwpTextInputV3Impl converted
them to an absolute range using the surrounding text it last sent, and
WaylandInputMethodContext then checked that range against the
SurroundingTextTracker's predicted state. These disagreed in two cases,
and the deletion was dropped with "The deletion range needs to cover
whole selection range.":

- The omnibox sets a reversed selection for inline autocompletion, and
  the absolute range was computed from the selection's start, which is
  its upper bound.
- Pending state is only sent after a done event whose serial matches
  the commit count, and wlroots does not send done for client commits.
  After a commit_string, the last sent surrounding text lags behind, so
  the range was computed from a stale cursor. Vietnamese Telex input in
  a web page produced "Vieẹt" instead of "Việt".

Forward the raw lengths to WaylandInputMethodContext and compute the
deletion around the predicted selection, or the preedit if present,
which already accounts for the input events applied since the
surrounding text was sent. This also removes the cached deletion range
bookkeeping from ZwpTextInputV3Impl.

The lagging surrounding text also hid the last commit from the input
method, so it could not compose with it, e.g. "ee" -> "ê". After
applying the input events of a done event, send the predicted
surrounding text before committing, as the protocol expects the state
sent in response to done to reflect the applied events. The update from
the text input client is then not sent again if it matches.
